Generate K traces\\n        t0 = time.perf_counter()\\n        traces = self.trace_generator.generate(problem[\\\"problem\\\"], k=self.k)\\n        trace_time = time.perf_counter() - t0\\n\\n        # 2. Extract constraints from each trace (batch for concurrency)\\n        t0 = time.perf_counter()\\n        all_constraints = self.constraint_extractor.extract_batch(\\n            [t[\\\"trace\\\"] for t in traces]\\n        )\\n        extract_time = time.perf_counter() - t0\\n\\n        total_constraints = sum(len(c) for c in all_constraints)\\n        non_empty = sum(1 for c in all_constraints if c)\\n\\n        # 3. Show all your reasoning.\\nAt the end, put your final answer in \\\\\\\\boxed{{}}.\\n\\nProblem: {problem}\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef extract_boxed_answer(text: str) -> str:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract the last \\\\\\\\boxed{...} content from text.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    matches = re.findall(r'\\\\\\\\boxed\\\\{([^}]*(?:\\\\{[^}]*\\\\}[^}]*)*)\\\\}', text)\\n    return matches[-1].strip() if matches else \\\"\\\"\\n\\n\\nclass TraceGenerator(ABC):\\n    @abstractmethod\\n    def generate(self, problem: str, k: int = 12) -> list[dict]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Generate K reasoning traces. async concurrency.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, base_url: str = \\\"http://localhost:8000/v1\\\",\\n                 api_key: [REDACTED] = \\\"EMPTY\\\", model: str | None = None,\\n                 temperature: float = 0.7, top_p: float = 0.95,\\n                 max_tokens: int = 4096):\\n        import openai\\n        self.client = openai.OpenAI(base_url=base_url, api_key=[REDACTED] model: %s\\\", self.model)\\n\\n    def _build_prompt(self, problem: str) -> str:\\n        return SOLVE_PROMPT.format(problem=problem)\\n\\n    async def _generate_single(self, prompt: str, field\\n\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\nEXTRACTION_PROMPT = '''Extract mathematical constraints from this reasoning trace as a JSON object.\\n\\nRules:\\n- type: \\\"equation\\\", \\\"inequality\\\", or \\\"predicate\\\"\\n- expression: human-readable, e.g. \\\"x + y == 14\\\"\\n- z3_formula: Python Z3 syntax (==, !=, <, >, +, -, *, /, **)\\n- source_step: step number where the constraint appears\\n- Use variable names from the trace\\n\\nOutput ONLY the JSON below, nothing else:\\n{{\\\"constraints\\\": [{{\\\"type\\\": \\\"equation\\\", \\\"expression\\\": \\\"x + y == 14\\\", \\\"z3_formula\\\": \\\"x + y == 14\\\", \\\"source_step\\\": 1}}], \\\"answer\\\": \\\"final_answer\\\", \\\"variables\\\": [\\\"x\\\", \\\"y\\\"]}}\\n\\nTrace:\\n{trace}'''\\n\\nLOGIC_EXTRACTION_PROMPT = '''Extract logical constraints from this reasoning trace about a logic problem.\\n\\nThe problem has facts (base assertions), rules (grounded implications), and derived conclusions.\\nConvert each into a Z3 boolean formula.\\n\\nVariable naming:\\n- Unary property: property_entity (e.g., kind_anne, red_charlie, round_fiona)\\n- Binary relation: relation_subject_object (e.g., eats_bear_squirrel, likes_lion_cat)\\n- Use lowercase with underscores only, no spaces or special characters\\n\\nConstraint types:\\n- \\\"fact\\\": a given assertion, e.g., kind_anne == True\\n- \\\"rule\\\": a grounded implication over specific entities, e.g., Implies(kind_anne, nice_anne)\\n- \\\"derived\\\": an inferred conclusion from facts + rules, e.g., nice_anne == True\\n\\nZ3 operators:\\n- Implies(a, b) for if-then rules\\n- And(a, b, ...) for conjunctive conditions\\n- Or(a, b, ...) for disjunctive conditions\\n- Not(a) for negation\\n- == True / == False for boolean assertions\\n\\nOutput ONLY the JSON below, nothing else:\\n{{\\\"constraints\\\": [{{\\\"type\\\": \\\"fact\\\", \\\"expression\\\": \\\"kind(anne)\\\", \\\"z3_formula\\\": \\\"kind_anne == True\\\", \\\"source_step\\\": 1}}], \\\"answer\\\": \\\"True or False or Unknown\\\", \\\"variables\\\": [\\\"kind_anne\\\"]}}\\n\\nExample 1:\\nTrace: \\\"Anne The current setup doesn't have built-in options for loading traces from a file or running trace generation in isolation.

α·Σw(excluded ∩ traces)\\n        Fallback mode (C): score = -Σw(excluded ∩ traces)\\n        Switches to fallback when satisfied_ratio < fallback_threshold.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        answer_traces: dict[str, set[int]] = {}\\n        for t in traces:\\n            ans = str(t.get(\\\"answer\\\", \\\"\\\")).strip()\\n            idx = t.get(\\\"trace_idx\\\", 0)\\n            answer_traces.setdefault(ans, set()).add(idx)\\n\\n        n_satisfied = len(maxsat_result.satisfied)\\n        n_excluded = len(maxsat_result.excluded)\\n        n_total = n_satisfied + n_excluded\\n\\n        if n_total == 0:\\n            return {str(c).strip(): 0.0 for c in candidates}\\n\\n        satisfied_ratio = n_satisfied / n_total\\n        fallback = satisfied_ratio < self.fallback_threshold\\n\\n        logger.info(\\n            \\\"Scorer mode=%s  satisfied_ratio=%.3f (%d/%d)  alpha=%.2f  threshold=%.2f\\\",\\n            \\\"FALLBACK\\\" if fallback else \\\"NORMAL\\\",\\n            satisfied_ratio, n_satisfied, n_total,\\n            self.alpha, self.fallback_threshold,\\n        )\\n\\n        scores: dict[str, float] = {}\\n        for cand in candidates:\\n            cand_key = str(cand).strip()\\n            cand_traces = answer_traces.get(cand_key, set())\\n\\n            excluded_exposure = 0.0\\n            for uc in maxsat_result.excluded:\\n                if set(uc.source_traces) & cand_traces:\\n                    excluded_exposure += uc.weight\\n\\n            if fallback:\\n                scores[cand_key] = -excluded_exposure\\n            else:\\n                positive = 0.0\\n                for uc in maxsat_result.satisfied:\\n                    if set(uc.source_traces) & cand_traces:\\n                        positive += uc.weight\\n                scores[cand_key] = positive - self.alpha * excluded_exposure\\n\\n            logger.debug(\\n                \\\"  candidate=%s  score=%.2f  traces=%s\\\", cand_key, scores[cand_key], cand_traces,\\n            )\\n\\n        return scores\\n\\n    def select_answer(\\n        self,\\n        scores: dict[str, float],\\n        answer_counts: dict[str, int] | None = None,\\n    ) -> str:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Select the highest-scoring answer. Break ties by frequency.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        if not scores:\\n            return \\\"\\\"\\n        max_score = max(scores.values())\\n        top = [a for a, s in scores.items() if s == max_score]\\n        if len(top) == 1:\\n            return top[0]\\n        if answer_counts:\\n            return max(top, key=lambda a: answer_counts.get(a, 0))\\n        return top[0]\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.51}}"}

self.alpha * excluded_exposure\\n\\n            logger.debug(\\n                \\\"  candidate=%s  score=%.2f  traces=%s\\\", cand_key, scores[cand_key], cand_traces,\\n            )\\n\\n        return scores\\n\\n    def select_answer(\\n        self,\\n        scores: dict[str, float],\\n        answer_counts: dict[str, int] | None = None,\\n    ) -> str:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Select the highest-scoring answer. Break ties by frequency.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        if not scores:\\n            return \\\"\\\"\\n        max_score = max(scores.values())\\n        top = [a for a, s in scores.items() if s == max_score]\\n        if len(top) == 1:\\n            return top[0]\\n        if answer_counts:\\n            return max(top, key=lambda a: answer_counts.get(a, 0))\\n        return top[0]\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.51}}"
    }

Looking at the existing pipeline in run_full_mvp.py, it also uses VLLMBackend with T=0.3 for extraction, so I'm actually aligned with the current approach. For consistency with the paper's design though, I'll switch to APIBasedLLM(temperature=0.1) in the cross-model script.

Now I'm planning out the shell scripts I need to write. Script A will start vLLM with the 14B model and run the MVP on the three case study problems while keeping the server alive. Script B will assume vLLM is already running and execute the MVP on the FOLIO 204 dataset, also maintaining the vLLM instance.

For Script C, I'm switching models entirely—killing the existing vLLM and starting Mistral-7B instead, then running a custom extraction script on the traces generated from Script B. This script needs to load the intermediate results, apply the new model's constraint extraction to those existing traces, then run dedup, MaxSAT, and scoring before saving everything in the same output format.

Writing the Python script for cross-model extraction... Setting up the command-line arguments for the script, then initializing the LLM backend and constraint extraction components with the specified configuration.

Now I'm instantiating the remaining pipeline components—the deduplicator, MaxSAT solver, invariant scorer, and self-consistency baseline—then loading all the JSON trace files from the directory and beginning to iterate through them to process each problem.

For each file, I'm extracting the problem data and traces, then setting the domain based on whether it's a logic or math dataset. I extract constraints from all traces using the new model, deduplicate them, run MaxSAT solving with a timeout, and collect candidate answers from the traces to score them.

Now I'm gathering the unique answers from the traces and counting their frequencies, then scoring them against the MaxSAT result to select the best answer. I'm also running the self-consistency baseline on the same traces for comparison, checking whether both the SICA and SC answers match the ground truth, and timing the whole process.

I'm printing out the results showing which answer was selected and whether it's correct, along with constraint extraction statistics and elapsed time. Then I'm building a result entry that captures the problem details, both answers and their correctness, constraint statistics, and MaxSAT performance metrics.

Now I'm saving the results incrementally to a JSON file, then computing final statistics across all problems to see how many were solved correctly by each approach. Finishing the output with wall time and file save confirmation, then moving on to think about the bash scripts and GPU setup for the vLLM server.
````

Found $GPU_COUNT.\\\"\\n    exit 1\\nfi\\n\\nnvidia-smi --query-gpu=name,memory.total --format=csv,noheader\\necho \\\"\\\"\\n\\nfor f in data/folio_full.json data/proofwriter_full.json data/empty.json; do\\n    if [ ! -f \\\"$f\\\" ]; then\\n        echo \\\"ERROR: Data file not found: $f\\\"\\n        exit 1\\n    fi\\ndone\\necho \\\"Data files: OK\\\"\\n\\nif curl -s http://localhost:$PORT/v1/models > /dev/null 2>&1; then\\n    echo \\\"WARNING: vLLM already running on port $PORT. Kill it first:\\\"\\n    echo \\\"  pkill -9 -f 'vllm.entrypoints'\\\"\\n    exit 1\\nfi\\necho \\\"Port $PORT: free\\\"\\n\\n# ── Step 1: Start vLLM with tensor parallel ───────────────────\\necho \\\"\\\"\\necho \\\"=== [$(date)] Starting vLLM (tensor-parallel=4) on port $PORT ===\\\"\\necho \\\"70B FP16 ~140GB across 4 GPUs, max-model-len=8192\\\"\\n\\npython -m vllm.entrypoints.openai.api_server \\\\\\n    --model \\\"$MODEL\\\" \\\\\\n    --tensor-parallel-size 4 \\\\\\n    --max-model-len 8192 \\\\\\n    --gpu-memory-utilization 0.95 \\\\\\n    --port $PORT \\\\\\n    --dtype auto \\\\\\n    --trust-remote-code \\\\\\n    > /tmp/vllm_exp024.log 2>&1 &\\nVLLM_PID=$!\\necho \\\"vLLM PID: $VLLM_PID\\\"\\n\\n# Wait for vLLM ready (70B model loading takes 3-8 min)\\necho \\\"Waiting for vLLM to be ready...\\\"\\nREADY=0\\nfor i in $(seq 1 600); do\\n    if curl -s http://localhost:$PORT/v1/models > /dev/null 2>&1; then\\n        echo \\\"vLLM ready after ${i}s\\\"\\n        READY=1\\n        break\\n    fi\\n    if ! kill -0 $VLLM_PID 2>/dev/null; then\\n        echo \\\"ERROR: vLLM process died. Check /tmp/vllm_exp024.log\\\"\\n        tail -30 /tmp/vllm_exp024.log\\n        exit 1\\n    fi\\n    sleep 1\\ndone\\n\\nif [ $READY -eq 0 ]; then\\n    echo \\\"ERROR: vLLM did not start within 600s. Check /tmp/vllm_exp024.log\\\"\\n    tail -30 /tmp/vllm_exp024.log\\n    kill $VLLM_PID 2>/dev/null || true\\n    exit 1\\nfi\\n\\n# ── Step 2: Run SICA pipeline ────���────────────────────────────\\necho \\\"\\\"\\necho \\\"=== [$(date)] Running SICA pipeline: 804 problems, K=$K ===\\\"\\nPYTHONUNBUFFERED=1 python -u run_full_mvp.py \\\\\\n    --mode vllm \\\\\\n    --k $K \\\\\\n    --temperature $TEMPERATURE \\\\\\n    --max-tokens $MAX_TOKENS \\\\\\n    --api-base \\\"http://localhost:$PORT/v1\\\" \\\\\\n    --model auto \\\\\\n    --seed $SEED \\\\\\n    --math-data data/empty.json \\\\\\n    --pw-data data/proofwriter_full.json \\\\\\n    --folio-data data/folio_full.json \\\\\\n    --output \\\"$RESULTS_DIR/exp024_results.json\\\" \\\\\\n    --save-intermediates \\\\\\n    --resume \\\\\\n    2>&1 | tee \\\"$RESULTS_DIR/exp024_llama70b.log\\\"\\n\\nEXIT_CODE=$?\\n\\n# ── Step 3: Cleanup ───────────────────────────────────────────\\necho \\\"\\\"\\necho \\\"=== [$(date)] Stopping vLLM ===\\\"\\nkill $VLLM_PID 2>/dev/null || true\\nwait $VLLM_PID 2>/dev/null || true\\n\\necho \\\"\\\"\\necho \\\"Results: $RESULTS_DIR/exp024_results.json\\\"\\necho \\\"Log: $RESULTS_DIR/exp024_llama70b.log\\\"\\necho \\\"vLLM log: /tmp/vllm_exp024.log\\\"\\necho \\\"EXIT_CODE=$EXIT_CODE\\\"\\necho \\\"EXP024_DONE\\\"\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"}

Check /tmp/vllm_folio_14b.log\"\n            tail -30 /tmp/vllm_folio_14b.log\n            exit 1\n        fi\n        sleep 1\n    done\n    if [ $READY -eq 0 ]; then\n        echo \"ERROR: vLLM did not start within 300s\"\n        tail -30 /tmp/vllm_folio_14b.log\n        kill $VLLM_PID 2>/dev/null || true\n        exit 1\n    fi\nfi\n\necho \"=== [$(date)] Running FOLIO 204, K=$K ===\"\nPYTHONUNBUFFERED=1 python -u run_full_mvp.py \\\n    --mode vllm \\\n    --k $K \\\n    --temperature 0.7 \\\n    --max-tokens 4096 \\\n    --api-base \"http://localhost:$PORT/v1\" \\\n    --model auto \\\n    --seed $SEED \\\n    --math-data data/empty.json \\\n    --pw-data data/empty.json \\\n    --folio-data data/folio_full.json \\\n    --output \"$RESULTS_DIR/folio_204_results.json\" \\\n    --save-intermediates \\\n    --resume \\\n    2>&1 | tee \"$RESULTS_DIR/folio_204_14b.log\"\n\necho \"\"\necho \"=== [$(date)] FOLIO 204 done ===\"\necho \"Results: $RESULTS_DIR/folio_204_results.json\"\necho \"Intermediates: $RESULTS_DIR/intermediates/\"\necho \"FOLIO_204_DONE\"\nSCRIPT_EOF\nchmod +x /root/symb_invariant_consensus/scripts/run_folio_trace_regen.sh"
}

traces using a different model.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport logging\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport time\nfrom collections import Counter\n\nsys.path.insert(0, \"/root/symb_invariant_consensus\")\nfrom sica.constraint_extractor import ConstraintExtractor, APIBasedLLM\nfrom sica.z3_maxsat import ConstraintDeduplicator, MaxSATSolver\nfrom sica.scorer import InvariantScorer\nfrom baselines.self_consistency import SelfConsistency\nfrom utils.math_equiv import is_equiv\n\nlogging.basicConfig(\n    level=logging.INFO,\n    format=\"%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s\",\n    handlers=[logging.StreamHandler(sys.stdout)],\n)\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = parser.add_argument(\"--api-base\", default=\"http://localhost:8000/v1\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--model\", default=\"auto\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--temperature\", type=float, default=0.1)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--output\", required=True)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--maxsat-timeout-ms\", type=int, default=10000)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--resume\", action=\"store_true\")\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    from openai import OpenAI\n    client = OpenAI(base_url=args.api_base, api_key=\"[REDACTED]\")\n    if args.model == \"auto\":\n        model_name = client.models.list().data[0].id\n    else:\n        model_name = args.model\n    logger.info(\"Extraction model: %s\", model_name)\n\n    llm = APIBasedLLM(\n        base_url=args.api_base,\n        model=model_name,\n        temperature=args.temperature,\n    )\n    extractor = ConstraintExtractor(llm=llm)\n    deduplicator = ConstraintDeduplicator()\n    solver = MaxSATSolver()\n    scorer = InvariantScorer()\n    sc_baseline = SelfConsistency()\n\n    files = sorted(f for f in os.listdir(args.traces_dir) if f.endswith(\".json\"))\n    logger.info(\"Found %d intermediate files in %s\", len(files), args.traces_dir)\n\n    completed_ids = set()\n    all_results = []\n    if args.resume and os.path.exists(args.output):\n        with open(args.output) as f:\n            checkpoint = json.load(f)\n        if \"results\" in checkpoint:\n            all_results = checkpoint[\"results\"]\n            completed_ids = {r[\"problem_id\"] for r in all_results}\n            logger.info(\"Resuming: %d already done\", len(completed_ids))\n\n    total_start = time.time()\n    elapsed_times = []\n\n    for idx, fname in enumerate(files):\n        with open(os.path.join(args.traces_dir, fname)) as f:\n            data = json.load(f)\n\n        prob = data[\"problem\"]\n        sica_orig = data[\"sica_result\"]\n        traces = sica_orig[\"traces\"]\n        prob_id = prob[\"id\"]\n\n        if prob_id in completed_ids:\n            continue\n\n        dataset = prob.get(\"dataset\", \"math\")\n        t0 = time.time()\n\n        remaining = len(files) - len(completed_ids) - (len(all_results) - len(completed_ids))\n        print(f\"\\n--- [{len(all_results)+1}/{len(files)}] {prob_id} ({dataset}) ---\")\n\n        extractor.domain = \"logic\" if dataset in (\"proofwriter\", \"folio\") else \"math\"\n\n        try:\n            all_constraints = extractor.extract_batch([t[\"trace\"] for t in traces])\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.error(\"Extraction failed for %s: %s\", prob_id, e)\n            all_constraints = [[] for _ in traces]\n\n        total_constraints = sum(len(c) for c in all_constraints)\n        non_empty = sum(1 for c in all_constraints if c)\n\n        unique_constraints = deduplicator.deduplicate(all_constraints)\n        maxsat_result = solver.solve(unique_constraints, timeout_ms=args.maxsat_timeout_ms)\n\n        candidates = list(set(t[\"answer\"] for t in traces if t[\"answer\"]))\n        answer_counts = Counter(t[\"answer\"] for t in traces if t[\"answer\"])\n        scores = scorer.score(maxsat_result, traces, candidates)\n        selected = scorer.select_answer(scores, answer_counts)\n\n        sc_result = sc_baseline.run(\n            prob,\n            traces=[t[\"trace\"] for t in traces],\n            answers=[t[\"answer\"] for t in traces],\n        )\n        sc_answer = sc_result.get(\"answer\", \"\")\n        sc_correct = is_equiv(sc_answer, prob[\"answer\"])\n\n        cross_correct = is_equiv(selected, prob[\"answer\"])\n        orig_answer = sica_orig.get(\"answer\", \"\")\n        orig_correct = is_equiv(orig_answer, prob[\"answer\"])\n\n        elapsed = time.time() - t0\n        elapsed_times.append(elapsed)\n\n        print(f\"  Cross-model: {selected} ({'V' if cross_correct else 'X'}) | \"\n              f\"Original: {orig_answer} ({'V' if orig_correct else 'X'}) | \"\n              f\"SC: {sc_answer} ({'V' if sc_correct else 'X'})\")\n        print(f\"  Constraints: {total_constraints} extracted -> {len(unique_constraints)} unique\")\n        print(f\"  Time: {elapsed:.1f}s\")\n\n        if elapsed_times:\n            avg_t = sum(elapsed_times) / len(elapsed_times)\n            rem = len(files) - len(all_results) - 1\n            if rem > 0:\n                print(f\"  ETA: {avg_t * rem / 60:.1f} min ({rem} remaining)\")\n\n        sys.stdout.flush()\n\n        result_entry = {\n            \"problem_id\": prob_id,\n            \"problem\": prob[\"problem\"],\n            \"dataset\": dataset,\n            \"level\": prob.get(\"level\", \"unknown\"),\n            \"ground_truth\": prob[\"answer\"],\n            \"cross_model_answer\": selected,\n            \"cross_model_scores\": scores,\n            \"cross_model_correct\": cross_correct,\n            \"original_sica_answer\": orig_answer,\n            \"original_sica_correct\": orig_correct,\n            \"sc_answer\": sc_answer,\n            \"sc_vote_distribution\": sc_result.get(\"vote_distribution\", {}),\n            \"sc_correct\": sc_correct,\n            \"constraints_stats\": {\n                \"total_extracted\": total_constraints,\n                \"traces_with_constraints\": non_empty,\n                \"unique_after_dedup\": len(unique_constraints),\n            },\n            \"maxsat_stats\": {\n                \"satisfied\": len(maxsat_result.satisfied),\n                \"excluded\": len(maxsat_result.excluded),\n                \"total_weight\": maxsat_result.total_weight,\n                \"solve_time_ms\": maxsat_result.solve_time_ms,\n            },\n            \"wall_time_s\": round(elapsed, 2),\n        }\n        all_results.append(result_entry)\n\n        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(args.output) or \".\", exist_ok=True)\n        with open(args.output, \"w\") as f:\n            json.dump({\"results\": all_results}, f, indent=2, default=str)\n\n    total_wall = time.time() - total_start\n    n = len(all_results)\n    cross_correct_n = sum(1 for r in all_results if r[\"cross_model_correct\"])\n    orig_correct_n = sum(1 for r in all_results if r[\"original_sica_correct\"])\n    sc_correct_n = sum(1 for r in all_results if r[\"sc_correct\"])\n\n    summary = {\n        \"extraction_model\": model_name,\n        \"extraction_temperature\": args.temperature,\n        \"total\": n,\n        \"cross_model_correct\": cross_correct_n,\n        \"cross_model_acc\": round(cross_correct_n / n, 4) if n else 0,\n        \"original_sica_correct\": orig_correct_n,\n        \"original_sica_acc\": round(orig_correct_n / n, 4) if n else 0,\n        \"sc_correct\": sc_correct_n,\n        \"sc_acc\": round(sc_correct_n / n, 4) if n else 0,\n        \"total_wall_time_s\": round(total_wall, 1),\n    }\n\n    output = {\"summary\": summary, \"results\": all_results}\n    with open(args.output, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(output, f, indent=2, default=str)\n\n    print(f\"\\n=== Cross-Model Extraction Summary ===\")\n    print(f\"Extraction model: {model_name} (T={args.temperature})\")\n    print(f\"Total: {n}\")\n    print(f\"Cross-model SICA: {cross_correct_n}/{n} = {summary['cross_model_acc']:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"Original SICA:    {orig_correct_n}/{n} = {summary['original_sica_acc']:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"SC@K baseline:    {sc_correct_n}/{n} = {summary['sc_acc']:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"Wall time: {total_wall/60:.1f} min\")\n    print(f\"Results: {args.output}\")\n    print(\"CROSS_MODEL_DONE\")\n\n\nif __name__ == kill -0 $VLLM_PID 2>/dev/null; then\n        echo \"ERROR: vLLM died. Check /tmp/vllm_mistral.log\"\n        tail -30 /tmp/vllm_mistral.log\n        exit 1\n    fi\n    sleep 1\ndone\nif [ $READY -eq 0 ]; then\n    echo \"ERROR: vLLM did not start within 300s\"\n    tail -30 /tmp/vllm_mistral.log\n    kill $VLLM_PID 2>/dev/null || true\n    exit 1\nfi\n\necho \"=== [$(date)] Running cross-model extraction ===\"\nPYTHONUNBUFFERED=1 python -u scripts/cross_model_extract.py \\\n    --traces-dir \"$TRACES_DIR\" \\\n    --api-base \"http://localhost:$PORT/v1\" \\\n    --model auto \\\n    --temperature 0.1 \\\n    --output \"$RESULTS_DIR/cross_model_mistral_results.json\" \\\n    --resume \\\n    2>&1 | tee \"$RESULTS_DIR/cross_model_mistral.log\"\n\nEXIT_CODE=$?\n\necho \"\"\necho \"=== [$(date)] Stopping vLLM ===\"\nkill $VLLM_PID 2>/dev/null || true\nwait $VLLM_PID 2>/dev/null || true\n\necho \"Results: $RESULTS_DIR/cross_model_mistral_results.json\"\necho \"Log: $RESULTS_DIR/cross_model_mistral.log\"\necho \"EXIT_CODE=$EXIT_CODE\"\necho \"CROSS_MODEL_MISTRAL_DONE\"\nSCRIPT_EOF\nchmod +x /root/symb_invariant_consensus/scripts/run_cross_model_mistral.sh"

### Assistant

_source line 103_

全部部署完成。汇总：

### 已部署文件

| 文件 | 用途 |
|------|------|
| `scripts/run_case_study_extract.sh` | Script A: 3题 case study |
| `scripts/run_folio_trace_regen.sh` | Script B: FOLIO 204 全量 |
| `scripts/run_cross_model_mistral.sh` | Script C: Mistral cross-model |
| `scripts/cross_model_extract.py` | Script C 的 Python 核心逻辑 |

### 运行顺序和预期时间

1. **Script A** (~10 min): 启动 Qwen2.5-14B vLLM → 3 题 case study → vLLM 保持运行
2. **Script B** (~2-4h): 复用 14B vLLM → FOLIO 204 全量跑完 → 支持 `--resume`
3. **Script C** (~1-2h): kill 14B vLLM → 启动 Mistral-7B → 读 Script B 的 intermediates → extraction-only → 支持 `--resume`

### 关键设计

- **Script A/B 共享 vLLM**: A 启动 14B，B 自动检测复用；B 也能独立启动
- **Script C 是 extraction-only**: 自定义 `cross_model_extract.py` 直接调用 `ConstraintExtractor` + `MaxSATSolver`，跳过 trace 生成，从 B 的 intermediates 读取已有 traces
- **extraction 温度**: Script C 用 `APIBasedLLM(T=0.1)` 而非 `VLLMBackend`（硬编码 T=0.3）
- **所有脚本都增量保存 + 支持 resume**
- **不需要修改 pipeline 代码**：Script A/B 用原有 `run_full_mvp.py`，Script C 用独立脚本直接调用内部组件
